- [How to Use Synology Cloud Sync to Encrypt and Decrypt Backblaze B2 Files](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-synology-cloud-sync-to-encrypt-and-decrypt-backblaze-b2-files): You can encrypt your data when you sync your Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage buckets to Synology Cloud Sync.
- [How to Use Snowflake to Query Existing Data in Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-snowflake-to-query-existing-data-in-backblaze-b2): You can create an external table to query an existing, partitioned, data set from Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage using Snowflake.
- [How to Use S5cmd with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-s5cmd-with-backblaze-b2): s5cmd is a free, open-source tool written in the Go language. It supports a wide range of object management tasks, both for cloud storage services and local file systems. It also supports parallel tasking and multiple threads for very fast throughput.
- [How to Use S3cmd with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-s3cmd-with-backblaze-b2): S3cmd is a free command-line tool and client for uploading, retrieving, and managing data in Amazon S3 and S3-compatible storage like Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Create a Cloud Storage Application using Python and Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-python-to-create-an-application): Backblaze provides procedures that demonstrate how to create a Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage application using Python.
- [Photos+ Cloud Library Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-photos-cloud-library-with-backblaze-b2): You can use Backblaze B2 with Photos+ Cloud library to backup your iOS photo library.

- [How to Create a Cloud Storage Application using Java and Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-java-to-create-an-application): Backblaze provides procedures that demonstrate how to create a Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage application using Java.

- [How to Use FileZilla Pro with Backblaze B2 for Cloud File Transfer](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-filezilla-pro-with-backblaze-b2): FileZilla Pro is an FTP tool that supports Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage. Using FileZilla Pro, you can quickly browse and transfer data into and out of your Backblaze B2 bucket.
- [How to Use Duplicacy to Upload Files to Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-duplicacy-to-upload-files-to-backblaze-b2): With Lock-Free Deduplication, Duplicacy works smoothly with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage without compromising any essential features that are required of a state-of-the-art backup tool.
- [How to Integrate Dell Isilon with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-dell-isilon-with-backblaze-b2): Dell Isilon is a Network Attached Storage (NAS) that supports multi-cloud services and is S3-compatible. You can configure Dell Isilon with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Create a Backblaze B2 Application and Manage Buckets with the CLI](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-cli-to-create-an-application): Backblaze provides procedures that demonstrate how to create a Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage application using the AWS command-line interface (AWS CLI).
- [How to Use ChronoSync for Mac with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-chronosync-with-backblaze-b2): ChronoSync is a third-party sync tool that runs on macOS and supports Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Manage Backblaze B2 Resources with Terraform](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-backblaze-b2-terraform): Terraform is an infrastructure as code (IaC) tool that was developed by HashiCorp. Instead of using imperative commands, Terraform uses declarative code. The Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Terraform provider lets you create and manage application keys, buckets, and upload files.
- [How to Forward Backblaze B2 Event Notifications from SNS to Lambda](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-aws-lambda-to-forward-event-notifications): This tutorial shows you how to deploy a serverless function in AWS Lambda to forward incoming event notification messages from Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage to an Amazon SNS topic or an Amazon SQS queue (depending on which of those options you choose) using the same message structure as Amazon S3.
- [How to Use Iconik Internet Storage Gateway with a Backblaze Fireball](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-use-a-backblaze-fireball-with-an-iconik-internet-storage-gateway): You can use a Backblaze Fireball with an iconik Internet Storage Gateway (ISG) setup.
- [Cloud Storage USB Snapshot Hard Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-usb-snapshot-hard-drive): You can monitor the status of submitted USB Snapshots from the Snapshots page of your Backblaze account. Learn everything about limit and shipments and how to handle your USB Snapshot Hard Drive order.
- [Options to Upload and Migrate Files to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-uploading-and-migrating-files): We cover the methods for direct uploads, how to handle backup and archive with your servers, media workflows that work with your existing NAS, and how storage and migration works.
- [How to Use the Native API for Uploading Files to Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-upload-files-with-the-native-api): This resource provides instructions for uploading files using the Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Native API.

- [How to Use Dropshare for Mac to Upload Files to Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-upload-files-to-backblaze-b2-with-dropshare): With Dropshare, you can upload screen shots, screen recordings, files, and more to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage and share it immediately with anyone.
- [How to Upload Files to a Synology NAS Using the Backblaze Fireball Program](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-upload-files-to-a-synology-nas-using-the-backblaze-fireball-program): The Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Fireball program consists of a Synology network attached storage (NAS) device with a 10GigE add-in card that can be filled in a few days.

- [How to Trigger Actions from Backblaze B2 Event Notifications (Webhooks)](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-trigger-an-action-from-an-event-notification): This tutorial uses Pipedream, a workflow automation platform that is similar to IFTTT and Zapier, to deploy the webhook endpoint, run JavaScript code to validate the message signature, and append a row to a Google Sheet that contains fields from the Backblaze B2 Event Notification message.

- [How to Back Up Your Synology NAS to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-synology-integrations): You can integrate Synology with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage for a seamless shared storage, backup, and collaboration solution.

- [Integrating the Snowflake Cloud Service with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-snowflake-integrations): Snowflake offers a cloud-based data storage and analytics service. Snowflake customers can manage and query data that is stored in Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [Creating and Managing Point-in-Time Snapshots in Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-snapshots): Snapshots are ZIP files that you can create from downloading files that are stored in a Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage bucket. Learn how to download many files for a single point in time.
- [How to Configure SSO for Okta in the Enterprise Web Console](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-set-up-sso-for-okta): These steps provide guidance for adding users to the Backblaze enterprise web console using single sign-on (SSO) through Okta.
- [How to Configure SCIM for Okta in the Enterprise Web Console](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-set-up-scim-for-okta): Integrating Okta with Backblaze enterprise web console allows for automated user provisioning.
- [How to Configure SCIM for Azure Entra in the Enterprise Web Console](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-set-up-scim-for-azure-entra): Integrating Microsoft Azure Entra with Backblaze enterprise web console allows for automated user provisioning.
- [How to Set Up Network-Attached Storage (NAS) with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-set-up-network-attached-storage-nas-with-backblaze-b2): Network-attached storage (NAS) is a file-level computer data storage device that you can connect to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Set Up Cloud Replication with the Backblaze B2 Command-Line Tool](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-set-up-cloud-replication-with-the-cli): If you have access to accounts that host both a source bucket and a destination bucket, you can set up Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Cloud Replication using the command-line interface.

Learn about redundancy, server-side encryption, TLS encryption, and data centers.
- [Self-Hosted Backup Integrations](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-self-hosted-backup-integrations): These comprehensive integration guides for self-hosted backup solutions enable you to maintain full control over your data protection strategy.
- [Cloud Storage Security](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-security): Learn how Backblaze safeguards your data to ensure the highest level of protection for your valuable files.
- [Community SDKs for the Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage API](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-sdks): Effortlessly integrate Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage into your applications with SDKs.
- [How to Save Files from Backblaze Computer Backup to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-save-files-from-computer-backup-to-cloud-storage): You can send files from Backblaze Personal Backup and Backblaze Business Backup to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage. Your files are stored securely in your private Backblaze B2 account as a decrypted .zip Snapshot until you delete the Snapshot from Backblaze B2.
- [S3-Compatible SDKs2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-s3-compatible-sdks): You can use the Backblaze S3-Compatible API with existing AWS SDKs. The following guides provide details about configuring the AWS SDKs. These SDKs make it easy to integrate your application no matter what language it is written in.
- [How to Use Backblaze B2 S3-Compatible App Keys](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-s3-compatible-app-keys): Application keys provide access to the Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage S3-Compatible API.
- [How to Use S3-Compatible API Bucket Versions in Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-s3-compatible-api-bucket-versions): Buckets that you store in Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage are versioned by default.
- [S3-Compatible API](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-s3-compatible-api): Use the Backblaze S3-Compatible API to interact with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [Cloud Storage Rules](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-rules): Learn how to apply rules to the data that is in your Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage account.
- [Restore a Time Machine Backup from Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-restore-a-time-machine-backup-from-backblaze-b2): If you previously stored a Time Machine backup in your Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage account, you can restore the backup on your Mac computer.
- [Cloud Storage Performance](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-resiliency-durability-and-availability): A Backblaze Vault consists of 20 storage pods, and the data is evenly spread across all 20 pods. Each storage pod in a given vault has the same number of drives and all of the drives are the same size.
- [How to Use Relink MSP360 Backups After a Cloud to Backblaze B2 Migration](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-relink-msp360-backups-after-a-cloud-to-backblaze-b2-migration): If you are an MSP360 customer who used a cloud migration tool, such as Flexify.IO, to move your data into Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage, you can sync your migrated cloud backup repository to your existing MSP360 backup files.
- [Recovery Integrations](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-recovery-integrations): These detailed integration guides for recovery solutions are designed to complement your cloud storage and backup systems. Whether you are restoring critical data, recovering applications, or ensuring business continuity after unexpected disruptions, these guides offer step-by-step instructions to seamlessly integrate recovery tools with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Use Veeam Virtual Machine Recovery with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-recover-veeam-virtual-machine-from-backblaze-b2): You can recover your data from Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage when you lose access to your primary backup environment and need to recover in a completely new Veeam Backup & Replication environment wherever that may reside.
- [Rate Limits](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-rate-limits): General information and guidance on Backblaze rate limiting.
- [Quickstart and Developer Guides](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-quick-start-guides): Quickly begin building applications that use Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage using our quick-start guides.
- [How to Integrate Quest with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-quest-integrations): Quest Rapid Recovery lets you protect anything—systems, apps, and data—anywhere, whether it is physical, virtual, or in the cloud. With Quest QoreStor, you can connect to the cloud for data archiving and disaster recovery faster and more easily, accelerate backup performance through protocol accelerators, and reduce storage requirements and costs through content-aware, variable block deduplication. You can integrate Quest with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [QNAP Systems Integrations](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-qnap-systems-integrations): QNAP can sync your Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage data to QNAP NAS devices.
- [Python Developer Quickstart Guide](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-python-developer-quick-start-guide): Python tutorial for building an application using the Backblaze S3-Compatible API.

- [Fastly Compute@Edge Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-fastly-compute-edge-with-backblaze-b2): Integrate Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage with Fastly Compute@Edge to enable serverless functions to run as close as possible to the end user.
- [Fastly CDN Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-fastly-cdn-with-backblaze-b2): The Fastly Content Delivery Network (CDN) lets you cache your data using multiple servers across the world, dramatically reducing the amount of latency involved with accessing it. You can set up Fastly CDN as a front end for your public Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage bucket.
- [ELEMENTS Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-elements-with-backblaze-b2): You can use with the ELEMENTS platform. ELEMENTS is an easy-to-use storage solution that is designed for media workflows.
- [EditShare FLOW Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-editshare-flow-with-backblaze-b2): EditShare FLOW is a media asset management solution that simplifies media sharing and collaboration with tools to manage media acquisition, assembly, and delivery. You can bridge your on-site storage and archives with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage to help users lay out their workflow and store their content in Backblaze B2.
- [How to Use Duplicati with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-duplicati-with-backblaze-b2): You can use Backblaze B2 to store your Duplicati backups.
- [Dell PowerProtect Data Domain Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-dell-powerprotect-data-domain-with-backblaze-b2): The Dell Cloud Tier feature of Dell PowerProtect Data Domain supports additional qualified S3 cloud providers, such as Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage, under an S3 flexible provider configuration option.
- [DataIntell Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-dataintell-with-backblaze-b2): DataIntell is an advanced analytics tool that provides useful insight into your storage. You can connect DataIntell with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Use Cyberduck for Windows with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-cyberduck-for-windows-with-backblaze-b2): You can integrate Cyberduck with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage on your PC.
- [How to Use Cyberduck for Mac with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-cyberduck-for-mac-with-backblaze-b2): You can integrate Cyberduck with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage on your Mac.
- [CuttingRoom Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-cuttingroom-with-backblaze-b2): CuttingRoom is a cloud-based video editor that you can connect with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Use CubeBackup with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-cubebackup-with-backblaze-b2): Use Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage with CubeBackup to back up Gmail, Google Drive, shared drives, contacts, calendars, and website data.
- [CTERA Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-ctera-with-backblaze-b2): CTERA is a cloud file storage and file system for Enterprises service that allows users to scale storage across a multi-tenant and cloud environment. You can configure CTERA with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Use CrossFTP with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-crossftp-with-backblaze-b2): CrossFTP is a free file transfer protocol (FTP) and cloud storage client for Windows, Mac, and Linux that you can configure with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Use Commvault with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-commvault-with-backblaze-b2): You can use Commvault to back up your files to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Use Cohesity with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-cohesity-with-backblaze-b2): Cohesity is a cloud-based data management tool that you can easily integrate with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [CloudSoda Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-cloudsoda-with-backblaze-b2): CloudSoda is a SaaS application that allows you to transfer data from an on-premise storage device (such as a network-attached storage (NAS), storage area network (SAN), or private cloud) to cloud object storage. This article provides the basics for configuring CloudSoda to transfer data between a NAS device and Backblaze B2 object storage.
- [CloudCasa Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-cloudcasa-with-backblaze-b2): You can use Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage with CloudCasa to set up a backup policy for your Kubernetes environment that sends data directly to your Backblaze B2 bucket.
- [How to Use Cloud Instant Backup Recovery for Veeam with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-cloud-instant-backup-recovery-with-backblaze-b2): Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage is a certified cloud object storage solution partner for Cloud Instant Backup Recovery (IBR). Cloud IBR is a fully-automated disaster recovery SaaS platform for Veeam backups that you store in Backblaze B2 providing an on-demand, automation-driven, bare-metal server, networking, and storage infrastructure.
- [How to Use Catalogic DPX with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-catalogic-dpx-with-backblaze-b2): Catalogic DPX was designed as a low-impact backup for physical and virtual environments. Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage is an official target for backup data with full support for Object Lock. You can integrate DPX with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage to protect against data loss and ransomware.
- [How to Use Catalogic DPX vPlus with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-catalogic-dpx-vplus-with-backblaze-b2): Catalogic DPX was designed as a low-impact backup for physical and virtual environments. DPX vPlus is an add-on product that addresses data protection gaps. You can integrate DPX vPlus with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage to protect against data loss and ransomware.
- [bunny.net Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-bunnynet-with-backblaze-b2): With Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage and bunny.net, you can share content without the worries of scalable performance tiers or limitations.
- [How to Use Bacula with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-bacula-with-backblaze-b2): You can integrate Bacula Enterprise with Backblaze B2 Cloud storage as an object endpoint in the cloud.
- [How to Use BackupGuard with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-backupguard-with-backblaze-b2): BackupGuard is a WordPress plugin that lets you back up your website to cloud services like Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Use BackupAssist with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-backupassist-with-backblaze-b2): BackupAssist can be used with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage to backup physical and virtual servers for Windows.
- [How to Use Arq 7 Immutable Backup with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-arq-7-immutable-backups-with-backblaze-b2): Arq is cloud backup software for Windows and Mac. Arq version 7 supports Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Object Lock with the new Immutable Backup option.
- [How to Use Arcserve UDP with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-arcserve-udp-with-backblaze-b2): Arcserve Unified Data Protection (UDP) lets you back up multiple systems from a centralized console and store those backups in S3-Compatible API storage like Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [Archiware P5 Companion Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-archiware-p5-companion-with-backblaze-b2): Archiware P5 is a software suite that lets you back up, archive, and sync media files.

- [Actifio Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-actifio-with-backblaze-b2): Actifio's Multi-Cloud Copy Data Management software lets users virtualize the copying of data and can be used with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Use Acronis Cyber Protect with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-integrate-acronis-cyber-protect-with-backblaze-b2): You can integrate the Acronis Cyber Protect platform with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.

From initial setup to advanced features and optimization, these resources empower you to maintain complete control over your files that are stored in Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.

- [How to Enable Object Lock or a Legal Hold on an Existing Bucket in Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-enable-object-lock-or-a-legal-hold-on-an-existing-bucket): Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Object Lock allows you to make your data immutable by preventing a file from being modified or deleted for a defined period of time. When configured, Default Bucket Retention applies Object Lock for a specified retention period on files that you upload or copy into a bucket.
- [How to Enable Live Read with the Backblaze B2 S3-Compatible API](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-enable-live-read-with-the-s3-compatible-api): Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Live Read provides the ability to read data from an object while that object is uploading.
- [How to Enable Encryption on a Bucket in Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-enable-encryption-on-a-bucket): Server-side encryption (SSE) protects your data by encrypting it before it is stored on disk by Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage. You can enable SSE on a bucket in the Backblaze UI.
- [How to Enable CORS with the Backblaze B2 S3-Compatible API](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-enable-cors-with-the-s3-compatible-api): Differences in the implementation of Cross-Origin Resource Sharing support in the S3-Compatible API are described here.

- [How to Enable and Manage CORS Rules in the Enterprise Web Console](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-enable-and-manage-cors-rules): With the Backblaze enterprise web console, you can enable cross-origin resource sharing (CORS) rules on B2 Cloud Storage buckets.

This means that content can be stored in Backblaze B2 while being delivered with the high speed and low latency of one of the world's premier CDNs. Users access your site with the same URLs as before, but now they enjoy the speed and reliability of Cloudflare.

- [Understand Backblaze B2 Data Caps and Alerts](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-data-caps-and-alerts): Read details about viewing and setting alerts and managing data caps. Data caps are are unlimited and unlimited charges can be accrued.
- [Custom Domain Installation Process](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-custom-domain-installation-process): You can attach a custom domain to a new or an existing Backblaze group. All of the members of a group must belong to a specific region. Group members are typically created using the Partner API, although you can also manually create them depending on the use case.

- [How to Create and Manage Groups in Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-create-and-manage-groups): You can create a Group, invite members to your Group, and manage your Group in the Backblaze UI.

- [How to Create and Manage Caps and Alerts in Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-create-and-manage-caps-and-alerts): With Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Caps and Alerts, you can limit the charges that occur per day due to storage, downloads, or transaction usage.

A Snapshot is a convenient way to download many files for a single point in time.
- [How to Create an Organization in the Enterprise Web Console](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-create-an-organization): The enterprise web console provides a centralized interface for managing your Backblaze B2 account at scale.

- [How to Create a Master App Key in the Backblaze B2 Partner API](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-create-a-master-app-key-for-the-partner-api): App keys control access to your groups in the Partner API.
- [How to Create a Lifecycle Rule with the Backblaze B2 Native API](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-create-a-lifecycle-rule-with-the-native-api): You can use the Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Native API to create Lifecycle Rules. Lifecycle Rules enable the Backblaze B2 service to automatically hide and delete older versions of your files that are stored in Backblaze B2.
- [How to Create a Lifecycle Rule with the Backblaze B2 Command-Line Tool](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-create-a-lifecycle-rule-with-the-cli): You can use the Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage Native command-line interface to create Lifecycle Rules. - [How to Create a Group in the Backblaze B2 Partner API](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-create-a-group-for-the-partner-api): You can create a Group and invite Group members to your groups using the Backblaze Partner API.

- [S3 Browser Integration with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-configure-s3-browser-with-backblaze-b2): S3 Browser is a freeware Windows Client for S3-compatible cloud services such as Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Use Rubrik with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-configure-rubrik-with-backblaze-b2): Rubrik is a software-defined data management platform for physical, virtual, and hybrid environments. - [How to Use Iperius Backup with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-configure-iperius-backup-with-backblaze-b2): You can use Iperius Backup to back up your files to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage. - [How to Use GoodSync with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-configure-goodsync-with-backblaze-b2): GoodSync integrates with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage to let you easily back up and sync your files. The secure file backup and synchronization software helps ensure that your files are never lost.
- [How to Configure Cache-Control Policies with the Backblaze B2 Native API](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-configure-cache-control-policies-with-the-native-api): Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage lets you configure cache-control policies on a bucket using the Native API.
- [How to Integrate Duplicity for Linux with Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-configure-backblaze-b2-with-duplicity-on-linux): Duplicity is an open-source backup tool that supports encryption, compression, and data retention. Duplicity can store backup data in many destinations, including Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.
- [How to Configure and Manage Lifecycle Rules in Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-configure-and-manage-lifecycle-rules): Lifecycle Rules enable Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage to automatically hide and delete older versions of your files. You can configure Lifecycle Rules on some of your files or all of the files in a bucket.
- [How to Use Recover Veeam Backup and Replication from a Scale-Out Backup Repository](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-configure-a-veeam-cloud-repository-recovery-from-backblaze-b2): You can use Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage as part of a Scale-Out Backup Repository when you lose access to your primary backup environment and need to recover it in a completely new Veeam Backup & Replication environment.
- [Cloud Compute Integrations](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-compute-integrations): These detailed integration guides for cloud compute solutions are designed to work seamlessly with your cloud storage and backup systems. The guides offer step-by-step instructions to connect and optimize your cloud compute environment with Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage.

Learn how to get yours.

You can back up storage volumes from CoreWeave to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage and access Backblaze B2 buckets from apps that run on CoreWeave.
- [How to Back up Linux to Backblaze B2](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-back-up-linux-to-backblaze-b2): Linux users can back up to the Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage service.

- [Cloud Storage Application Keys](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-application-keys): You can create app keys that are limited to a bucket. These are useful because they continue to work even when you change the master app key.
- [Cloud Storage Application Key Capabilities](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-application-key-capabilities): A comprehensive list of each application key (app key) and their associated capabilities, providing access to Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage APIs.

- [API Operations](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-api-operations): This resource provides a list of all of the Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage APIs and links to their corresponding documentation.

- [How to Add and Manage Users in the Enterprise Web Console](https://www.backblaze.com/docs/cloud-storage-add-and-manage-users): You can manage users in the Backblaze enterprise web console by setting up a system for cross-domain identity management (SCIM) or enabling single sign-on (SSO).

- [View Files Backed Up Using Extended Version History](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/view-files-backed-up-using-extended-version-history): Files that have not backed up with Backblaze Computer Backup in more than 30 days or files were removed from your computer longer than 30 days ago will not appear in your current backup view. If you have Backblaze Extended Version History, you can roll back time to locate those files.
- [View Backed-Up Files](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/view-backed-up-files): You can use a web browser to view the files that you back up to Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Version History](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/version-history): Backblaze backs up the data files that are currently on your computer and selected external hard drives. If a backed-up file is modified or deleted, Backblaze retains a copy of the pre-modification or previously deleted file for a period of time before removing it from your backup in a data center.
- [Verify an Email Address](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/verify-an-email-address): To use all of the features of the Backblaze web UI, you must verify the email address that you used to sign up for your account.
- [Use Version History to Remove Backup Data](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-version-history-to-remove-backup-data): If you enabled Extended Version History on your Backblaze Computer Backup account and you need to cycle your data out before the time that is designated by your Extended Version History, you can downgrade your Version History either permanently or temporarily.
- [Use The Unarchiver to Unzip a Restore File (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-the-unarchiver-to-unzip-a-restore-mac): If the default Mac utility fails when you unzip your backup file from Backblaze Computer Backup, you can use the free Mac app The Unarchiver.
- [How to Transfer Your Backblaze License to a New Computer](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-the-transfer-a-license-option): You can remove a Backblaze Computer Backup from an account and reassign the license to another backup in the same account.
- [How to Use the Reports Option (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-the-reports-option-mac): The last tab in the Mac version of the Backblaze Client settings contains several valuable reports that are related to Backblaze backups.
- [How to Use the Reports and Issues Options (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-the-reports-and-issues-options-windows): The last tab in the Windows version of the Backblaze Client settings contains several valuable reports that are related to Backblaze backups.
- [How to Use the Backblaze Inherit Backup State Option When You Migrate to a New Computer](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-the-inherit-backup-state-option): You can use an Inherit Backup State to resume your old Backblaze backup on your new computer.
- [Use the Command-Line Interface to Hide the Backblaze Icon](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-the-command-line-interface-to-hide-the-backblaze-icon): The Backblaze Computer Backup icon in the system tray lets you access the Backblaze control panel. Administrators can hide the Backblaze icon.
- [How to Use Microsoft Group Policy to Install the Backblaze Client](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-microsoft-group-policy-to-install-the-backblaze-client): Microsoft’s Group Policy management system for Windows Domains can simplify the process of installing Backblaze Computer Backup on multiple computers.
- [Use Info-ZIP to Unzip a Restore File (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-info-zip-to-unzip-a-restore-file-windows): You can use the Windows built-in unzipping utility to unzip a Backblaze Computer Backup restore. You also have the option to use Info-ZIP to unzip your restore files.
- [How to Use Explainfile to Diagnose Backup Issues (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-explainfile-to-diagnose-backup-issues-windows): Backblaze provides a diagnostic tool called "Explainfile" that can help you troubleshoot files that were not accurately backed up. This article shows you how to use this utility for Windows.
- [How to Use Explainfile to Diagnose Backup Issues (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-explainfile-to-diagnose-backup-issues-mac): Backblaze provides a diagnostic tool called "Explainfile" that can help you troubleshoot files that were not accurately backed up. This article shows you how to use this utility on a Mac.
- [Use BetterZip to Unzip a Restore File (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-betterzip-to-unzip-a-restore-file-mac): In some cases, the default Mac unzipping utility may fail while unzipping your Backblaze Computer Backup restore file. In these cases, Backblaze recommends that you use an alternative tool, such as BetterZip.
- [Use and Manage Enterprise Control](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-and-manage-enterprise-control): Administrators can use Backblaze Enterprise Control to manage Backblaze Computer Backup for your organization. Enterprise Control lets you control a group member's backed-up data, as well as which options that group members can control, such as restoring and removing backups.
- [How to Use a USB Restore Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-a-usb-restore-drive): Your Backblaze restore drive arrives encrypted, and you must unlock it to access the data.
- [Apply a Discount Code or a Prepaid Code](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-a-prepaid-code): You can use a prepaid code to pay for Backblaze Computer Backup. You can use a discount code to apply a promotional or special offer to reduce the total cost of your Computer Backup license.
- [Use 7-Zip to Unzip a Restore File](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/use-7-zip-to-unzip-a-restore-file): Learn how to unzip a Backblaze Computer Backup restore file with 7-Zip.
- [USB Restore Drive Size](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/usb-restore-drive-size): When you order a restore hard drive from Backblaze, the size of your restore determines the size of the hard drive that you will receive.
- [Update the Backblaze Backup Client](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/update-the-backblaze-client): There are three options to update the Backblaze Computer Backup client to the newest version. This article provides information about using these options.
- [Manage Billing](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/update-billing): You can use the Backblaze web console to change your existing Backblaze Computer Backup billing to a new credit card.
- [Update an Account on Behalf of a Member](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/update-an-account-on-behalf-of-a-member): As a Backblaze Business Group admin, you can update a member's password, email address, or phone number on their behalf. However, you must know the user's current password to make these changes, and you must have access to the member's phone number and be able to receive SMS messages to change the account phone number. As an admin, you cannot delete a member's account or remove the member's account from the Group while you are logged in as the user.
- [Unzip a Restore File (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/unzip-a-restore-file-windows): When you restore data from Backblaze Computer Backup, your files are packaged and compressed into a ZIP file. Learn how to unzip a Backblaze ZIP file in Windows.
- [Unzip a Restore](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/unzip-a-restore): There are multiple options to unzip a restore file. The articles in this section provide more information about those options and how to use them.
- [Unsupported Operating Systems](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/unsupported-operating-systems): If you use an operating system that is not supported by Backblaze, then your backups can either not properly function or not back up altogether. Backblaze recommends that you always update to the most current supported version of your operating system.
- [Uninstall the Backblaze Client Silently (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/uninstall-the-backblaze-client-silently-windows): You can silently uninstall the Backblaze Computer Backup client from a Windows computer.

- [How to Completely Uninstall and Remove Backblaze from Your Mac](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/uninstall-backblaze-mac): There are two methods you can use to uninstall Backblaze Computer Backup on your Mac computer.
