If you still have issues, you can contact Backblaze Support for help.
- [Troubleshoot Account Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/troubleshoot-account-issues): You can resolve Backblaze Computer Backup account-related concerns swiftly using the articles in this section.
- [Troubleshoot a Restore](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/troubleshoot-a-restore): This collection of articles provide information to troubleshoot issues that you may encounter when you restore your data from Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Troubleshoot a Backup](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/troubleshoot-a-backup): The following articles provide information to troubleshoot issues you may encounter when you back up your data in Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Transferring a Paid License](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/transferring-a-paid-license): You can move a paid Backblaze Computer Backup license from your original computer to a new computer or installation using two methods: Inherit Backup State or Transfer License. Both methods properly license the new installation, but the way in which it is handled differs.
- [Supported Operating Systems](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/supported-operating-systems): Backblaze Computer Backup provides client software and support for multiple operating systems.
- [Supported Backup Data](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/supported-backup-data): What does Backblaze back up? Backblaze backs up all of your data across all of the user profiles that are on your computer as soon as you install the client.
- [SSL Connection](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/ssl-connection): Backblaze Computer Backup uses a secure sockets layer (SSL) connection to encrypt your data and send it to the Backblaze data center.
- [Share a File](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/share-a-file): You can share individual files from your Backblaze account.
- [Settings and System Preferences](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/settings-and-system-preferences): This collection of articles helps you configure the Backblaze client for optimization, permissions, performance, and more.
- [Set Up Extended Version History with Groups](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/set-up-extended-version-history-with-groups): If your Backblaze client is on version 7.0 or newer, you can increase the version history for a business group from 30-days (included) to either one year or forever.
- [Set Up Extended Version History](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/set-up-extended-version-history): Backblaze retains a 30-day version history of your files. After 30 days, the updated, changed, or deleted versions are removed from your Backblaze backup, leaving only the most recent, current version of your files. You can add one year of version history to retain all of the versions of your backed-up files for one year after you modify or delete them from your device. This remains the case as long as you have this option selected and your account is in good standing. You can also purchase "forever" version history to retain modified or deleted file versions beyond one year.
- [How to Set Permissions on the Backblaze Folder (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/set-permissions-on-the-backblaze-folder-windows): Issues getting your changes in the Backblaze Computer Backup software settings to persist may be caused by file permissions on the Backblaze folder. Backblaze writes to the settings file when you make a change. This article shows you how to resolve this issue.
- [How to Set Permissions on the Backblaze Folder & Fix Verifying Disk Permissions (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/set-permissions-on-the-backblaze-folder-mac): Incorrectly set permissions can prevent Backblaze from writing to the settings file when you make a change. This article shows you how to configure those settings.
- [Set a Temporary Data Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/set-a-temporary-data-drive): Backblaze Computer Backup creates a temporary copy of large data files during the backup process. If space is limited on your computer's main hard drive, you can use an external hard drive for this purpose.
- [How to Set a Backup Schedule (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/set-a-backup-schedule-windows): You have multiple options when you set up a backup schedule in the Backblaze Computer Backup client for Windows.
- [How to Set a Backup Schedule (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/set-a-backup-schedule-mac): You have multiple options when you set up a backup schedule in the Backblaze Computer Backup client for Mac.
- [How to Send Logs to Backblaze (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/send-logs-to-backblaze-windows): You can send logs to Backblaze Support in order to help troubleshoot an issue with Backblaze Computer Backup on your Windows computer.
- [How to Send Logs to Backblaze (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/send-logs-to-backblaze-mac): You can send logs to Backblaze Support in order to help troubleshoot an issue with Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Select a Data Region](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/select-a-data-region): When you create a Backblaze Computer Backup account, you choose whether that account’s data is stored in the US East region, the US West region, or the EU Central region. The choice that you make during account creation dictates where all of that account’s data is stored.
- [Backblaze Security, Redundancy, and Cloud Storage Data Protection](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/security): Backblaze is committed to keeping your data secure. Backblaze encrypts all files that are scheduled for backup, sends those encrypted files over an encrypted connection, and stores them encrypted in a secure data center that has both physical and electronic security.
- [Safety Freeze](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/safety-freeze): The Safety Freeze feature protects you from losing your backed-up data when errors occur. If Backblaze detects a major inconsistency between the backup records on your computer and the records on servers, Backblaze "freezes" the state of your backup.
- [How to Return a USB Restore Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/return-a-usb-restore-drive): If you do not need to keep a Backblaze USB drive after you restore a large amount of data, you can return the drive within 30 days and receive a refund, effectively making the process of restoring free!
- [Retain Backups During Extended Leaves](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/retain-backups-during-extended-leaves): If you go on vacation or your computer is going to be shut off for an extended period, you may wonder what happens to your backed-up data. This article provides best practices for leaving your computer or your external hard drives.
- [How to Resume an Offline Computer](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resume-an-offline-computer): If Backblaze Computer Backup has not backed up your data in a long time, you may see a message indicating that Backblaze is offline. A common cause of this issue is a firewall on your computer that prevents Computer Backup from backing up your data.
- [How to Resume a Missing Computer](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resume-a-missing-computer): If your computer is active and connected to the internet, but it has not backed up to Backblaze Computer Backup in a long time, you may receive an email from Backblaze that indicates that your computer is missing. This article shows you how to resume your missing computer.
- [Restore Your Data](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-your-data): Restoring backed-up data to your computer is a crucial aspect of the backup process and ensures that your files and information can be recovered in the event of data loss. The restoration process involves retrieving and copying the backed-up data from its storage location back to your computer. You can restore your data in Backblaze Computer Backup from a ZIP file or from a USB hard drive.
- [Restore Windows Live Mail](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-windows-live-mail): You can use Backblaze Computer Backup to restore and download your Windows Live Mail Files.
- [Restore Quicken (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-quicken-mac): You can use Backblaze Computer Backup to recover Quicken files on your Mac computer.
- [Restore Quickbooks Files](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-quickbooks-files): You can download your Quickbooks files from your Backblaze Computer Backup just like any other files.
- [Restore Microsoft Outlook](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-microsoft-outlook): If you use Microsoft Outlook on a Windows computer, your Outlook data files are stored in a specific location on your computer. This data is typically backed up by Backblaze Computer Backup, and you can recover these files from your online backup like any other files.
- [Restore iTunes (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-itunes-windows): If you use the iTunes application on your Windows computer, the files are stored in your computer's file system. This data is typically backed up by Backblaze Computer Backup, and you can recover these files from your online backup like any other files.
- [Restore iTunes (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-itunes-mac): If you use the iTunes application on your Mac computer (Mojave and earlier), the files are stored in your computer's file system. This data is typically backed up by Backblaze Computer Backup, and you can recover these files from your online backup like any other files.
- [Restore Firefox Bookmarks](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-firefox-bookmarks): For Windows 8.1 or Windows 10, Firefox locally stores bookmarks that are backed up by Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Restore Files to a Different Computer](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-files-to-a-different-computer): When you back up your data with Backblaze Computer Backup, you can access it on a different computer.
- [Restore Files and Applications](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-files-and-applications): The articles in this section provide information about using your Backblaze Computer Backup restore after it is complete.
- [Restore Apple TV and Apple Movies](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-apple-tv-and-apple-movies): If you use macOS Catalina or newer, the Apple TV application is typically used to watch movies and TV shows. If you save movies and TV shows on your Mac, the files are stored in your Mac's file system. This data is typically backed up by Backblaze Computer Backup, and you can recover these files from your online backup just like any other files.
- [Restore Apple Podcasts](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-apple-podcasts): If you use macOS Catalina or newer, the Podcasts application is typically used to listen to podcasts. If you save podcast episodes on your Mac, the podcast files are stored in your Mac's file system. This data is typically backed up by Backblaze Computer Backup, and you can recover these files from your online backup like any other files.
- [Restore Apple Music](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-apple-music): You can use Backblaze Computer Backup to restore and download your Apple Mail files. This article shows you how to restore your Apple Music Library on Catalina and newer operating systems.
- [Restore Apple Mail](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-apple-mail): You can use Backblaze Computer Backup to restore and download your Apple Mail files.
- [Restore Apple Books](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-apple-books): If you use the Apple Books application on your Mac computer, the information that is used by the application is stored in a specific location on your computer. This data is typically backed up by Backblaze Computer Backup, and you can recover these files from your online backup like any other files.
- [Restore Apple Address Book](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-apple-address-book): If you use the Apple Address Book application on your Mac computer, the addresses, phone numbers, and other information that are used by the application are stored in a specific location on your computer. This data is typically backed up by Backblaze Computer Backup, and you can recover these files from your online backup like any other files.
- [Restore App Release Notes (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-app-release-notes-windows): This article provides release notes for the Backblaze Computer Backup Restore App for Windows.
- [Restore App Release Notes (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/restore-app-release-notes-mac): This article provides release notes for the Backblaze Computer Backup Restore App for Mac.
- [Resolve Two-Factor Verification Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-two-factor-verification-issues): This article helps you resolve issues with Backblaze Computer Backup two-factor verification.
- [How to Resolve the Horse Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-the-horse-error): You might receive the error code "Horse" while attempting to inherit a backup state for Backblaze Computer Backup. This article helps you resolve that error.
- [Resolve the Fish Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-the-fish-error): You might receive an error message with the word fish when you run the Backblaze Computer Backup installer. This is usually caused by a temporary interruption in your internet connection, or your security software may be interfering with the installation.
- [How to Resolve the Drive Letter Alert](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-the-drive-letter-alert): On your Windows computer, you may see an alert message from Backblaze Computer Backup which asks you to rename the drive letter for your external hard drive. You can resolve this issue by assigning a stable drive letter to the drive.
- [Resolve the BzSelfExtractor Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-the-bzselfextractor-error): If you install Backblaze Computer Backup on a Windows computer, you may receive a BzSelfExtractor error message. This error indicates that the security software on your computer (such as a firewall or antivirus software) is interfering with the installation process.
- [Resolve the Bear Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-the-bear-error): You might receive an error message with the code word "bear" when you run the Backblaze Computer Backup installer. This is usually caused by a temporary interruption in the Backblaze data center, or your security software may be interfering with the installation.
- [How to Resolve Skipped Files](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-skipped-files)
- [How to Resolve Security Certificate Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-security-certificate-issues): Due to a widely used internet certificate that expired on September 30th, 2021, if your operating system is older, you may need to manually update your certificate to access the Backblaze website.
- [Resolve Run Command, Creditpipe failed, and errB Errors](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-run-command-creditpipe-failed-and-errb-errors): You might receive the error message Error Run Command, creditpipe failed, err=B when you run the Backblaze Computer Backup installer. This usually indicates that the security software on your computer (such as a firewall or antivirus software) is interfering with the installation process.
- [How to Resolve Permission Errors](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-permission-errors): This article helps you resolve the MacOS error "The folder ... can't be opened because you don't have permission to see its contents" that you may see when you view the contents of a USB restore drive.
- [How to Resolve Performance Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-performance-issues): Learn how to adjust Backblaze's speed throttle to mitigate bandwidth slowdowns.
- [How to Resolve Menu Icon Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-menu-icon-issues): If the Backblaze Computer Backup icon does not appear in the menu bar of your computer, you can display it by changing your Computer Backup settings.
- [How to Resolve Low Disk Space](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-low-disk-space): This article helps you resolve the message Your Computer Is Low On Disk Space. By default, Backblaze backs up individual files to an unlimited size. You can reduce this size in your Backblaze settings. If your system hard drive has free space that is less than 1.1 times the size of the largest file that is scheduled to be backed up, Backblaze does not back up that file and sends an alert to the user.
- [How to Resolve iOS Mobile App Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-ios-mobile-app-issues): This article provides troubleshooting steps to resolve issues when using the Backblaze iOS mobile app.
- [How to Resolve Internet Explorer Restore Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-internet-explorer-restore-issues): If you use Internet Explorer and you are unable to download a restore file from Backblaze Computer Backup, the issue may be due to an option in Internet Explorer that blocks file downloads from websites that use HTTPS. You can disable this option to resolve the issue.
- [Resolve Installer Permission Failures (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-installer-permission-failures-windows): If you install Backblaze Computer Backup on Windows, you may encounter an error message depending on your computer's security settings. This article provides instructions to resolve this issue.
- [What to Do When Your Backblaze External Drive Is Not Backing Up](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-external-hard-drive-issues): This article provides instructions to resolve issues with external hard drive backups.
- [How to Resolve Encryption Software Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-encryption-software-issues): Third-party encryption programs may offer a setting that allows you to mount a drive as removable media. You may have to deselect this option to allow your drives to back up to Backblaze.
- [How to Resolve Empty Files After a Restore (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-empty-files-after-a-restore-windows): If you unzip a Backblaze Computer Backup restore with the built-in Windows unzip utility, you may see empty, invalid, or 0 KB files. The best way to resolve this issue is to use an alternate unzipping tool.
- [How to Resolve Delays Finding External Drives or New Files](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-delays-finding-external-drives-or-new-files): When you create new files or change files on your computer or plug in an external drive that includes new or changed files, it may take up to two hours for Backblaze Computer Backup to schedule the files for online backup. You can trigger a scan of your computer.
- [Resolve Common Installation Failures](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-common-installation-failures): This article provides some common causes of installation failures that you may be able to fix yourself.
- [How to Resolve Antivirus Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-antivirus-issues): You may need to exclude Backblaze Computer Backup from some anti-virus programs, even if they are set to disabled because it continues to block Backblaze processes.
- [Resolve an Issue Fetching your Account](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-an-issue-fetching-your-account): You may encounter an error message when you log in to the Backblaze Computer Backup website. This article provides the steps to resolve this error.
- [Resolve an Expired Trial Alert](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-an-expired-trial-alert): Each new installation of the Backblaze Backup Client creates a unique ID and backup. This prevents a new installation or reinstallation of the Backup Client from accidentally overwriting an existing backup. This new installation runs as a 15-day trial and begins a new initial backup. When this trial is nearing expiration, you receive an alert.
- [How to Resolve a Synchronization Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-synchronization-error): You may receive the error: After synchronizing with the Backblaze datacenter, this machine is still not licensed. There are a few solutions to license the new installation. You should choose the option that best works for your specific case.
- [How to Resolve a Safety Freeze](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-safety-freeze): A Safety Freeze protects you from losing your data that is backed up in Backblaze Computer Backup when errors occur.
- [How to Resolve a Read-Writable .bzvol Directory Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-read-writable-bzvol-directory-error): When you select a drive for a backup, Backblaze Computer Backup creates a unique hidden folder called .bzvol at the root of each hard drive that you back up. Then a bzvol_id.xml file is created inside of that folder. If Computer Backup cannot create the folder, it displays the message: "Backblaze could not create a read-writable '.bzvol' directory on that hard drive."
- [Resolve a Public/Private Key Mismatch](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-publicprivate-key-mismatch): This article shows you how to resolve a public or private key mismatch issue.
- [How to Resolve a Paused Backup](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-paused-backup): This article shows you how to resolve Backblaze pausing but still showing 'Initial Backup' even though you have zero files remaining.
- [How to Resolve a Missing or Duplicate .bzvol Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-missing-or-duplicate-bzvol-error): Backblaze Computer Backup creates a unique file called .bzvol at the root of each hard drive that is backed up. This file helps identify the drives that are included in the backup. If this file is damaged or missing, it can cause errors.
- [How to Resolve a Missing Drive Issue](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-missing-drive-issue): With Backblaze Computer Backup, drives are required to be connected a minimum of once every 30 days to be maintained in your backup. If a drive has been disconnected for some time or cannot be detected, you may see a message in the Backblaze Web UI or the Backblaze Client.
- [How to Resolve a MainDialog TopBanner Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-maindialog-topbanner-error): This article shows you how to resolve the ERR_MainDialog_TopBanner error.
- [Resolve a Forgotten Private Encryption Key](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-forgotten-private-encryption-key): You can add a private encryption key (PEK) to help secure your data stored in Backblaze Computer Backup. If you do this, you must remember your key. There is no way for Backblaze or anybody else to recover your key. Without the key, your data is unreadable and you must delete the unreadable backup to free the license for use with a new backup
- [How to Resolve a Duplicate Mount Point](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-duplicate-mount-point): Learn how to resolve a duplicate mount point error in macOS X.
- [How to Resolve a Duplicate Hard Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-duplicate-hard-drive): Backblaze Computer Backup software writes unique files to the top of each drive in order to identify the drive in your backup. If there is a problem identifying a drive, this can cause the drive to be shown twice in Computer Backup settings. This article shows you how to resolve this issue.
- [How to Resolve a Drive Update Issue](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-drive-update-issue): There are a few issues that can prevent a drive from updating in Backblaze Computer Backup. This article provides the most common instances and how to correct them.
- [Resolve a Custom Installer Asking Users for a Password](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-custom-installer-asking-users-for-a-password): If you manage a Backblaze Computer Backup account for a group of users, a user may report that Computer Backup is asking for a password when they run the custom installer.
- [How to Resolve a bzserv Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-bzserv-error): This article provides instructions to resolve the message: WARNING: The bzserv service is not running properly. Please download and install a new Backblaze client to resolve this problem.
- [How to Resolve the 'bzfileids.dat file is too large' Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-bzfileidsdat-file-error): Backblaze Computer Backup maintains a .dat file on your computer. When backing up your files, the bzfileids.dat file is loaded into RAM to run a checksum before uploading your files to the server. If this file get too large, you may receive an error. This article provides instructions to resolve this issue.
- [How to Resolve a Bzbui Error](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/resolve-a-bzbui-error): You might receive the following message on your computer: Bzbui is not working properly. This indicates that security software on your computer (such as a firewall or antivirus software) is interfering with Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Reset an Account Password](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/reset-an-account-password): You can change or reset your Backblaze account password with the Backblaze Web UI.
- [Rename a Backup on an Account](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/rename-a-backup-on-an-account): You can change the name of your backup through the {{variable.Backblaze}} client that is installed on your computer.
- [Remove a License from an Account](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/remove-a-license-from-an-account): If you have an unused license on your Backblaze Computer Backup account, you can remove it from your account to reduce your bill.
- [Release Notes](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/release-notes): This article provides release notes for the Backblaze Computer Backup Client, Restore app, and Downloader app.
- [Refer a Friend](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/refer-a-friend): The Backblaze Refer-A-Friend Program lets you give your friends a month of Backblaze Computer Backup for free. Also, for every friend who successfully purchases a Backblaze Computer Backup subscription, no matter which plan they choose, Backblaze discounts the equivalent of one month’s charge on your next renewal.
- [Processes that Run as Part of the Software](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/processes-that-run-as-part-of-the-software): Backblaze Computer Backup runs several system processes on your computer that are required for various functions of the online backup software. These processes are required for proper operation.
- [Preview Image Files](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/preview-image-files): Image files that you back up with Backblaze Computer Backup display a preview of the image when you view them in the Backblaze web console.
- [Partition a Time Machine Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/partition-a-time-machine-drive): To avoid duplicating data, Backblaze Computer Backup does not back up a hard drive that contains Time Machine data. If you want to use the drive to back up data to Backblaze Computer Backup, you must change how Time Machine uses the drive.
- [Order a USB Restore Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/order-a-usb-restore-drive): You can order a restore hard drive that contains the data that you backed up to your Backblaze Computer Backup account. The restore drive is shipped to your address, and you can return the drive to Backblaze for a refund within 30 days of receiving the drive.
- [Order a Mac Restore Drive for Windows](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/order-a-mac-restore-drive-for-windows): You can use your Mac-formatted Backblaze Computer Backup restore drive on a Windows machine by creating a restore as a B2 Snapshot and selecting the NTFS drive option.
- [Optimize Settings for Faster Uploads](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/optimize-settings-for-faster-uploads)
- [Network-Attached Storage (NAS) Devices](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/network-attached-storage-nas-devices): Backblaze does not support backing up a network drive, network share, or network-attached storage (NAS) device using Backblaze Computer Backup due to potentially high operating costs. However, you can use the Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage service to back up NAS devices offsite.
- [How to Move Files from a Restore to a Local Hard Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/move-files-from-a-restore-to-a-local-hard-drive): A Backblaze restore is a container for your requested files. Because of this, there is no automatic function to restore the files to your computer. However, moving the files from your restore to your hard drive is very simple.
- [Move a Group Member](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/move-a-group-member): Managing group members by efficiently moving them between different groups.
- [Migrate Data with Migration Assistant](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/migrate-data-with-migration-assistant): If you have a Mac computer and want to use the Migration Assistant software to move your files to a new computer, you should follow these steps to ensure that Backblaze Computer Backup does not encounter a safety freeze.
- [Migrate Data](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/migrate-data): Backblaze Computer Backup is designed to retain snapshots of your computer on servers, and not for rapid data transfer between devices. You can use Backblaze to migrate data, but it may not be the best solution if you need to move data quickly, or if you want to move system files or application data. In many cases, you can better accomplish this using third-party services and operating system utilities. If you decide to manually transfer data locally, having your data backed up with Backblaze first gives you an extra off-site copy of your data in case of failure during migration.
- [Manage Enterprise Control](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/manage-enterprise-control): With Backblaze Enterprise Control you can manage backups and restores for users in your Group.
- [Manage a Member's Account](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/manage-a-members-account): As an administrator of a Backblaze Business Group, you can sign in to a member's account to view backups and restore a backup.
- [Mac Settings and System Preferences](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/mac-settings-and-system-preferences): This article is an overview of Backblaze system preferences for Mac.
- [Locate the Backblaze Client Version](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/locate-the-backblaze-client-version): You can locate your Backblaze Computer Backup client version in the About screen.
- [Locate My Computer](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/locate-my-computer): Backblaze can help recover your lost computer or stolen laptop by tracking it if it is connected to a network again. You can disable the Locate My Computer feature if you do not want Backblaze to access your computer's location.
- [How to Locate Data from Failed or Disconnected Hard Drives](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/locate-data-from-failed-or-disconnected-hard-drives): You can use the Backblaze web console to locate files from disconnected or failed secondary hard drives.
- [Locate a userPub file for Troubleshooting (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/locate-a-userpub-file-for-troubleshooting-windows): A userPub.pem file is unique to each computer backup. You may need to provide this file to the Backblaze Support team to troubleshoot your backup for Windows.
- [Locate a userPub file for Troubleshooting (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/locate-a-userpub-file-for-troubleshooting-mac): A userPub.pem file is unique to each computer backup. You may need to provide this file to the Backblaze Support team to troubleshoot your backup on your Mac.
- [Locate a Group Administrator](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/locate-a-group-administrator): You can locate the administrator for your group from two places in the Backblaze web UI. Both of the options display a Group Information dialog that includes contact and admin information and a list of your services.
- [Lightroom Preview Files](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/lightroom-preview-files): To avoid unnecessary use of your bandwidth, Backblaze Computer Backup does not back up Adobe Lightroom's preview files. If you need to restore your Lightroom files, the .lrdata files are automatically recreated from the other Lightroom .lrcat files.
- [License a Trial Installation](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/license-a-trial-installation): You can apply a paid license to your Backblaze Computer Backup trial installation.
- [Leave a Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/leave-a-group): You can leave a Backblaze Computer Backup business group at any time.
- [Join a Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/join-a-group): You can enable Backblaze Business Groups in the UI and easily join that account.
- [Invite Members to a Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/invite-members-to-a-group): You can create a Business Group in the Backblaze UI and invite members to that group.
- [International Customers](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/international-customers): Learn how to optimize Backblaze Computer Backup as an international customer.
- [How to Download and Install the Backblaze Client for Windows](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/install-the-backup-client-windows): You can install the Backblaze Computer Backup client on your Windows operating system to begin backing up your data.
- [How to Download and Install the Backblaze Client on your Mac](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/install-the-backup-client-mac): You can install the Backblaze Computer Backup client on your MacOS to begin backing up your data.
- [Install the Backblaze Client](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/install-the-backup-client): This collection of articles shows you how to install the Backblaze Computer Backup client for both Mac and Windows. You an also install the client silently.
- [How to install the Backblaze Client using a Different Account](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/install-the-backblaze-client-using-a-different-account): When you uninstall and reinstall the Backblaze Backup Client, it remembers past account information. However, you can install the Backup Client under a different email address.
- [How to install the Backblaze Client Silently with Kandji (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/install-the-backblaze-client-silently-with-kandji-mac)
- [How to install the Backblaze Client Silently with Jamf (Decentralized)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/install-the-backblaze-client-silently-with-jamf)
- [How to install the Backblaze Client Silently (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/install-the-backblaze-client-silently-windows): For Microsoft’s line of Windows operating systems, pushing an application to hundreds of users generally involves an MSI package installer. The installers have a mechanism for copying to and running on supported machines, either by itself or as part of a script. The Backblaze Computer Backup MSI installer, when run on a user account, can determine the user’s email address from the domain’s Active Directory.
- [How to install the Backblaze Client Silently (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/install-the-backblaze-client-silently-mac): For Apple's line of macOS operating systems, pushing an application to hundreds or thousands of users generally involves a mountable DMG file that contains an installer package. The Backblaze Computer Backup Installer for macOS works similarly. There are a just few unique steps to deploy the client silently.
- [How to install the Backblaze Client Silently for Mojave (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/install-the-backblaze-client-silently-for-mojave-mac): With Apple Mojave, Apple added security to parts of the computer it considers sensitive or critical. Applications that require sensitive access to these parts of the system now require additional permissions. Full disk access, something that only a low-level disk maintenance tool or backup clients require, needs these permissions. A silent install now requires an additional component, a custom policy configuration that grants the Backblaze Computer Backup client full disk access.
- [Identify Users with Full Disk Access (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/identify-users-with-full-disk-access-mac): Using your Group admin account, you can view the usage report to locate the Mac users who granted full disk access to Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Get Started](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/get-started): After you sign up for a Backblaze Computer Backup account, you can easily install the client on your computer to automatically back up your data. After installation, you can select specific hard drives to back up, set your backup schedule, or change your performance settings.
- [File Sizes](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/file-sizes): By default, Backblaze does not have file size restrictions. This article provides information about how large files are uploaded and how file sizes are calculated.
- [File Backup and Restoration Time Frames](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/file-backup-and-restoration-time-frames): The time it takes to complete your initial backup with Backblaze Computer Backup and to restore a backup using a web download depends on the speed of your internet connection.
- [External Hard Drives](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/external-hard-drives): During installation, Backblaze backs up any USB or Firewire hard drive that you have attached to your computer. You can also add external hard drives to Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Encryption](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/encryption): Protecting the privacy of your data is a top priority for Backblaze. This article provides the process that Backblaze uses to encrypt your data.
- [Enable Groups](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/enable-groups): Enable the Backblaze Groups feature to manage, pay for, and administrate several users.
- [https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/enable-and-disable-two-factor-verification](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/enable-and-disable-two-factor-verification)
- [Enable and Disable Single Sign-On (SSO) for a Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/enable-and-disable-single-sign-on-sso-for-a-group): Single sign-on (SSO) is a popular solution to maintain security for Admins while providing ease-of-use to end users. SSO allows a user to log in once and access services without re-entering authentication factors. Backblaze currently allows SSO for GSuite and Office365.
- [Enable and Disable Single-Sign On (SSO)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/enable-and-disable-single-sign-on-sso): Backblaze Computer Backup supports single sign-on (SSO) as a way to add additional security for your account.
- [How to Enable Accessibility Options (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/enable-accessibility-options-windows): Dictation is an accessibility feature that you can enable on Windows computers and use when interacting with the Backblaze Computer Backup Support team.
- [How to Enable Accessibility Options (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/enable-accessibility-options-mac): Dictation is an accessibility feature that can be enabled on Mac computers and used when interacting with the Backblaze Computer Backup Support team.
- [Email Alerts](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/email-alerts): This article explains when backup email alerts are sent.
- [Edit a Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/edit-a-group): After you create a Backblaze Business Group, you can edit that group in the user interface.
- [Downloader App Release Notes (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/downloader-app-release-notes-windows): This article provides release notes for the Backblaze Computer Backup Downloader App for Windows.
- [Downloader App Release Notes (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/downloader-app-release-notes-mac): This article provides release notes for the Backblaze Computer Backup Downloader App for Mac.
- [How to Download a Zipped Restore File (Web UI)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/download-a-zipped-restore-file-web-ui): You can use the Backblaze Web UI to download the Zipped file that you created as a restore.
- [How to Download a Single File (Web UI)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/download-a-single-file-web-ui): You can restore your files that are backed up on Backblaze Computer Backup by downloading them directly from the Backblaze web UI.
- [How to Download a Restore Using the Downloader App for Windows](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/download-a-restore-using-the-downloader-app-windows): You can use the Backblaze Downloader application to download large ZIP file restores.
- [How to Download a Restore Using the Backblaze Downloader App for Mac](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/download-a-restore-using-the-downloader-app-mac): To increase the speed and reliability when downloading a restore over 2 GB, use the Backblaze Downloader application. With this app, you also have the ability to resume a download if it is interrupted.
- [Download a Restore](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/download-a-restore): There are multiple ways to download a restore. The articles under this topic provide the necessary information to complete a restore.
- [Disable Time Machine and Back Up to Backblaze](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/disable-time-machine-and-back-up-to-backblaze): To avoid duplicating data, Backblaze Computer Backup does not back up a hard drive that contains Time Machine data. To back up that drive to Backblaze, you must discontinue using Time Machine, delete its backups, and select it for backup to Backblaze.
- [How to Disable Location Services to Resolve a Windows Issue](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/disable-location-services-to-resolve-a-windows-issue): If a location services error appears for your Windows operating system, you may need to allow the bzbui file in your Location Privacy and Security settings.
- [Disable Groups on an Account](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/disable-groups-on-an-account): You can disable groups on a Backblaze Computer Backup account depending on your specific scenario.
- [How to Disable a Firewall](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/disable-a-firewall): This article provides links to temporarily disable popular firewalls to resolve issues with Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Delete Uploaded Files](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/delete-uploaded-files): The only way to immediately delete all uploaded files is to delete your entire backup. This article provides links to more information about deleting backups and exclusion settings.
- [How to Delete Files from a USB Restore Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/delete-files-from-a-usb-restore-drive): If you receive a Western Digital My Passport or an 8 TB Western Digital My Book restore drive from Backblaze, you can erase the drive before you return it to Backblaze or repurpose the drive.
- [Delete a Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/delete-a-group): You can delete a Backblaze Business Group in the user interface. Group coverage for member's backups are terminated, but member's accounts are not deleted.
- [Delete a Backup for a Managed User](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/delete-a-backup-for-a-managed-user): As the Administrator of a Backblaze Managed Group, you can delete backups on behalf of your Group members.
- [Delete a Backup](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/delete-a-backup): You can easily delete an extra or unwanted Backblaze backup from your preferences using the web UI.
- [Deduplication](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/deduplication): Deduplication refers to a process in Backblaze Computer Backup that prevents files from being unintentionally duplicated in your online backup.
- [Guide to Backblaze Data Centers and Regions](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/data-centers-and-data-regions): This article provides information about the data centers and data regions in which your files are stored in Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Create an Account with Groups Enabled](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-an-account-with-groups-enabled): You can create a Backblaze account that includes an enabled group.
- [How to Create a System Information Report (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-a-system-information-report-windows): A Backblaze Support agent may request a system report if you have an issue on your Windows computer. You can generate a system report from the System Information program on your computer.
- [Create a Restore (Web UI)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-a-restore-web-ui): You can create a Backblaze Computer Backup restore of your backed-up files using the Backblaze Web UI.
- [Create a Restore on Behalf of a Group Member](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-a-restore-on-behalf-of-a-group-member): With Backblaze Computer Backup managed groups, administrators can create restores for group members.
- [Create a Restore of the Photos Library (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-a-restore-of-the-photos-library-mac): The Photos app on a Mac computer stores its data in a package file. The package contains the original image files for your photos, plus associated metadata. This article shows you how to restore those files.
- [Create a Restore of Mac OS Packages](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-a-restore-of-mac-os-packages): You can create a Backblaze Computer Backup restore of a full Mac OS package or files within the package.
- [Create a Restore of Deleted or Previous File Versions](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-a-restore-of-deleted-or-previous-file-versions): You can use Backblaze Computer Backup to restore deleted or previous versions of your files that you backed up to Backblaze's servers.
- [Create a Restore (Client)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-a-restore-client): Version 9.0 of the Backblaze Computer Backup application has a built-in restore application that lets you download files directly to Macs that run macOS 11 Big Sur and above, or PCs running Windows 7 and above.
- [Create a Restore](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-a-restore): You can create a Backblaze Computer Backup restore using either the Backblaze web UI or the Backblaze client.
- [Create a Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/create-a-group): You can create a business group in the Backblaze web console. Groups let you to manage, pay for, and administrate several users.
- [How to Contact the Backblaze Computer Backup Support Team](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/contact-the-support-team): You can access the Backblaze support chat Monday through Friday.
- [How to Configure Security Settings (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-security-settings-windows): Backblaze encrypts all data during transmission and storage in a Backblaze data center. You can apply an additional level of encryption to your backup in the Security settings by adding a Private Encryption Key (PEK).
- [How to Configure Security Settings (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-security-settings-mac): Backblaze encrypts all data during transmission and storage in a Backblaze data center. You can apply an additional level of encryption to your backup in the Security settings by adding a Private Encryption Key (PEK).
- [How to Configure Power Settings (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-power-settings-windows): You can optimize Backblaze Computer Backup power settings for Windows.
- [How to Configure Power Settings (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-power-settings-mac): You can optimize Backblaze Computer Backup power settings on your Mac.
- [How to Configure Performance Settings (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-performance-settings-windows): This article explains how to configure Backblaze Computer Backup performance settings for Windows.
- [How to Configure Performance Settings (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-performance-settings-mac): This article explains how to configure Backblaze Computer Backup performance settings on a Mac.
- [Configure Groups](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-groups): You can create and manage Groups using the Backblaze web UI to manage, pay for, and administrate several users.
- [How to Configure General Settings (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-general-settings-windows): The Backblaze Computer Backup settings are broken up into categories, and each category has its own tab. The Settings tab contains the general Backblaze backup settings. This article provides information about Windows general settings.
- [How to Configure General Settings (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-general-settings-mac): The Backblaze Computer Backup settings are broken up into categories, and each category has its own tab. The Settings tab contains the general Backblaze backup settings. This article provides information about Mac general settings.
- [How to Configure Exclusions (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-exclusions-windows): Backblaze Computer Backup is designed to back up your entire computer. This article provides the information you need to back up only certain folders for Windows.
- [How to Configure Exclusions (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-exclusions-mac): Backblaze Computer Backup is designed to back up your entire computer. This article provides the information you need to back up only certain folders for Mac.
- [Configure Enterprise Control](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-enterprise-control): Administrators can use Backblaze Enterprise Control to manage Backblaze Computer Backup for their organization.
- [How to Configure Custom Exclusions using XML (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-custom-exclusions-using-xml-windows): Exclusions prevent all files and subfolders of a directory from being indexed or included in a backup. Learn how to set custom exclusions using XML on your Windows machine.
- [How to Configure Custom Exclusions using XML (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-custom-exclusions-using-xml-mac): Exclusions prevent all files and subfolders of a directory from being indexed or included in a backup. Learn how to set custom exclusions using XML on your Mac.
- [How to Configure an AVG Firewall to Resolve Operation Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-an-avg-firewall-to-resolve-operation-issues): Security programs like AVG can incorrectly flag the Backblaze Computer Backup software as malware. To resolve this issue, configure AVG to allow Backblaze to back up your data.
- [How to Configure an Avast Firewall to Resolve Operation Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-an-avast-firewall-to-resolve-operation-issues): Depending on its configuration, Avast's firewall may block Backblaze from operating correctly. This article shows you how to resolve this issue.
- [How to Configure a Bitdefender Firewall to Resolve Operation Issues](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/configure-a-bitdefender-firewall-to-resolve-operation-issues): Depending on its configuration, Bitdefender's firewall may block Backblaze Computer Backup from operating correctly.
- [How to Collect a Processes List (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/collect-a-processes-list-windows): For troubleshooting issues, you may need to provide a Support agent with a list of processes that are currently running on your Windows computer.
- [How to Collect a Processes List (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/collect-a-processes-list-mac): For troubleshooting issues, you may need to provide a Support agent with a list of processes that are currently running on your Mac computer.
- [Cloned Hard Drives](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/cloned-hard-drives): Learn about some popular cloning options and how to exclude the .bzvol file so that Backblaze Computer Backup functions properly.
- [Check the Status of a Restore Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/check-the-status-of-a-restore-drive): After you order a restore drive from Backblaze, you can check the status of your drive shipment in the Backblaze web UI.
- [Change the Frequency of Backup Summary Emails](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/change-the-frequency-of-backup-summary-emails): When you sign up for a Backblaze Computer Backup account, a backup summary email is sent to you once a month, by default. You can opt out of these emails or receive them on a weekly basis instead.
- [Change an Account Email Address](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/change-an-account-email-address): You can change the email address that is associated with your Backblaze Computer Backup account.
- [How to Cancel or Delete your Backblaze Account](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/cancel-or-delete-a-backblaze-account): The steps to cancel a Backblaze Computer Backup account depend on the services that are enabled on the account.
- [Computer Backup Best Practices](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/best-practices): This article provides suggestions to support a successful online backup strategy using Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Backup Schedule](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/backup-schedule): Backblaze is designed to be lightweight on your computer's resources. Backblaze automatically adjusts the upload rate and performance to keep the load off your network and your system's resources.
- [Backup Client Release Notes (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/backup-client-release-notes-windows): This article provides release notes for the Backblaze Computer Backup Backup Client for Windows.
- [Backup Client Release Notes (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/backup-client-release-notes-mac): This article provides release notes for the Backblaze Computer Backup Backup Client for Mac.
- [Backblaze Mobile App](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/backblaze-mobile-app): The Backblaze mobile app is available for iOS and Android. There is no additional charge to download, install, and use the Backblaze mobile app on as many devices as you want.
- [Backblaze IP Addresses](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/backblaze-ip-addresses): All of the traffic for Backblaze services use IP addresses in the same ranges. This article provides those ranges in CIDR notation.
- [Backblaze Groups](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/backblaze-groups): The Backblaze Groups feature lets you manage, pay for, and administrate several users. Groups allow several people to have individual accounts that all fall under one credit card and renew on the same date. If the Group is managed, the administrators also have access to manage user's accounts, create restores, and perform any action a user can typically perform on their account. Groups is a great feature for all sizes of business or families who want to be able to manage payment for multiple members.
- [Back Up Your Data](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-your-data): Backing up your computer is a crucial practice to ensure the safety of your data. With Backblaze Computer Backup, you can back up computers and laptops in the cloud to protect all of your documents from hardware failure, accidents, or ransomware. This collection of articles helps you to back up data, view your backed-up data, manage data, migrate data, delete backed up files, and more.
- [Back Up Virtual Machines](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-virtual-machines): If you use a virtual machine on your Mac computer, you can back it up with Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Back Up TrueCrypt Containers](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-truecrypt-containers): Backblaze Computer Backup can back up TrueCrypt containers if you change the preferences to preserve the modification timestamp on file containers.
- [Back Up Third-Party Application Data](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-third-party-application-data): You can back up data from many third-party applications to Backblaze Computer Backup.
- [Back Up Select Folders](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-select-folders): Backblaze Computer Backup is designed to back up your entire computer by default. You can back up only certain folders by setting Backblaze to exclude all of the folders that you do not want to back up.
- [Back Up OneDrive (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-onedrive-windows): Online-only and locally available files are assigned reparse point flags. Therefore, you must disable OneDrive if you want the files that are in the OneDrive folder to back up to Backblaze.
- [Back Up OneDrive (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-onedrive-mac): OneDrive's Files on Demand feature prevents OneDrive from being accessed by Backblaze Computer Backup, so this feature must be disabled to back up your OneDrive folder.
- [Back Up MAMP (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-mamp-mac): You can back up your MAMP data to Backblaze Computer Backup. By default, MAMP uses the Applications folder on a Mac to store unique user data.
- [Back Up iCloud Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-icloud-drive): You can back up iCloud Drive files to Backblaze Computer Backup as long as the files are saved locally on your computer and are not stored solely on iCloud Drive remote servers.
- [Back Up Google Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-google-drive): Google Drive has settings in place that can prevent it from backing up to Backblaze Computer Backup. Files that are stored only in the cloud will not back up to Backblaze, but you can configure Google Drive to ensure that it can back up.
- [Back Up FileVault](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-filevault): You can back up FileVault to Backblaze Computer Backup if your Mac computer is unlocked and FileVault is enabled.
- [Does Backblaze Back Up External Hard Drives?](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-external-hard-drives): During installation, Backblaze backs up any USB or Firewire hard drive that you have attached to your computer. You can also add external hard drives using Mac or Windows.
- [Back Up Box Drive](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-box-drive): Learn about alternative options to back up your Box Drive files.
- [How to Back Up an External Hard Drive for Windows](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-an-external-hard-drive-windows): You can use Backblaze Computer Backup to back up your external hard drives using the Backblaze Windows client.
- [How to Back Up an External Hard Drive on a Mac](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-an-external-hard-drive-mac): You can use Backblaze Computer Backup to back up your external hard drives using the Backblaze Mac client.
- [How to Install Backblaze and Back Up a New Computer](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-a-new-computer): To begin backing up a new computer under your existing Backblaze account, sign in to your Backblaze account and install the Backblaze client for your specific operating system.
- [Back Up a Boot Camp Partition](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/back-up-a-boot-camp-partition): If you use Backblaze Computer Backup on your Mac, you may also want to back up your files that are stored on a Windows partition that was created with Boot Camp.
- [Assign an Unused License](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/assign-an-unused-license): If you purchased a license for your Backblaze Computer Backup account for a new installation, you can apply it to a backup two ways.
- [Administer a Group Account](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/administer-a-group-account): Administrators can see account details for Backblaze Business Group members and alerts that are on their accounts. Administrators have equal rights and can change group settings and add or remove other administrators.
- [Add Services to a Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/add-services-to-a-group): You can add services to a new or existing business group using the Backblaze web UI.
- [How to Add or Remove a Blocked Wi-Fi Network (Windows)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/add-or-remove-a-blocked-wi-fi-network-windows): You can prevent Backblaze Computer Backup from backing up on selected Wi-Fi networks by adding those networks to a block list. If you add a Wi-Fi network to the block list, some Backblaze data may still be transmitted, but Backblaze will not upload backup data while you are connected to that network. This article shows you how to add and remove these connections for Windows.
- [How to Add or Remove a Blocked Wi-Fi Network (Mac)](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/add-or-remove-a-blocked-wi-fi-network-mac): You can prevent Backblaze Computer Backup from backing up on selected Wi-Fi networks by adding those networks to a block list. If you add a Wi-Fi network to the block list, some Backblaze data may still be transmitted, but Backblaze will not upload backup data while you are connected to that network. This article shows you how to add and remove these connections for a Mac.
- [Add or Change Group Billing](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/add-or-change-group-billing): You can easily add billing to a Group, add an ACH payment method, or change Group billing using the Backblaze web UI.
- [How to Add Backblaze to Windows Defender Allowances](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/add-backblaze-to-windows-defender-allowances): You can add Backblaze Computer Backup to Windows Defender's white list, to prevent Windows Defender from blocking Backblaze.
- [How to Add Backblaze to Bitdefender's Allowances](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/add-backblaze-to-bitdefenders-allowances): Security programs like Bitdefender may incorrectly flag the Backblaze Computer Backup software as malware. To resolve this issue, add a rule to Bitdefender to allow Backblaze to back up your data.
- [Add an Administrator to a Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/add-an-administrator-to-a-group): When you create a Backblaze business group, you have the option to add administrators to the group other than yourself. You can also add more administrators after you create the group.
- [How to Back Up Multiple Computers to a Backblaze Account](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/add-a-new-computer-to-an-account): You can back up multiple computers under the same Backblaze account. You must purchase a license for every computer that you back up.
- [Add a License to a Business Group](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/add-a-license-to-a-business-group): You can add licenses to a Backblaze Business Group using two different methods.
- [Account Security](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/account-security): Backblaze takes your account security seriously by providing multiple account authentication options.
- [Account Management & Administration](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/account-management-administration): Explore Backblaze Computer Backup account management and administration features, such as setting up a Group and managing Enterprise Control.
- [About Backblaze Computer Backup](https://www.backblaze.com/computer-backup/docs/about-backblaze-computer-backup): Backblaze Computer Backup is designed to back up all of your data on Mac and Windows computers. This backup covers unlimited data on the computer itself as well as internal drives and external drives that are physically connected to the computer. Backblaze Computer Backup is an active, evolving mirror of the current state of your computer and drives. By default, all Computer Backup accounts include version history. You also have the option to upgrade to an extended version history that allows you to retain deleted data or disconnected drive data for an extended period of time.

- [Upload a Part in a Multipart Upload by Copying Data](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-upload-part-copy): Utilizes an existing object in the cloud as a data source to upload part of a multipart large file upload.
- [Upload One Part in a Multipart Large File Upload](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-upload-part): Starts uploading one part in a multipart file upload. The multipart upload must be initiated before using this command.
- [Add an Object Retention Configuration to an Object](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-put-object-retention): Creates an Object Retention configuration and then applies that configuration to a specific Backblaze object.
- [Set Default Object Lock Configurations in a Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-put-object-lock-configuration): Applies a specific Object Lock configuration to a bucket, which will apply to all future objects in that bucket.
- [Apply a Legal Hold Configuration to an Object](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-put-object-legal-hold): Creates a Legal Hold configuration that a specified object in the cloud will be attached to in the future.
- [Sets the ACL Permissions for an Object in a Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-put-object-acl): Creates a default Access Control List (ACL) permission for new or existing objects that are contained within a bucket.
- [Upload an Object Directly to Your Backblaze Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-put-object): Allows you to upload an object to a Backblaze bucket, which creates a cloud copy that you can retrieve in the future.
- [S3 Put Bucket Logging](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-put-bucket-logging)
- [Set the Default Encryption Configuration on a Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-put-bucket-encryption): Creates a default encryption configuration that your bucket will automatically utilize if no other is specified.
- [Set the CORS on an Existing Backblaze Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-put-bucket-cors): Sets the CORS configuration on your Backblaze bucket; this command will overwrite any previous configurations.
- [Set the ACL on an Existing Backblaze Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-put-bucket-acl): Allows you to set the ACL on an existing Backblaze bucket, which you can do with a specific header request.
- [S3 List Parts](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-list-parts)
- [Another Way to List Objects Within a Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-list-objects-v2): Returns a list of objects within a bucket, with the ability to return more than 1,000 objects if needed.
- [List Objects Currently Within Your Backblaze Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-list-objects): Returns a full list of objects within a Backblaze bucket. The maximum length of the list is 1,000 objects.
- [List Metadata of Versions of Objects in a Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-list-object-versions): Provides information about the metadata of various versions of your objects, including past versions.
- [List In-Progress Multipart Uploads Currently Processing](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-list-multipart-uploads): Provides a list of all multipart uploads in your account that have not been either completed or aborted.
- [List All Buckets in Your Backblaze Account](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-list-buckets): Returns a full list of the buckets within your Backblaze account. The buckets will be listed in alphabetical order.
- [Gather Metadata About an Existing Object](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-head-object): Allows you to pull metadata information about your object without actually having to return the object itself.
- [Learn Whether or Not a Bucket Exists in Your Account](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-head-bucket): Allows you to request information about a bucket. You can search for a bucket by name to see whether it exists.
- [Determine an Object's Current Retention Settings](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-object-retention): Gives information about the current retention settings of an object, related to the Object Lock configurations.
- [Determine the Object Lock Configuration of a Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-object-lock-configuration): Provides information about the configuration of the Object Lock options within a specific Backblaze bucket.
- [Retrieve an Object's Legal Hold Status from Backblaze](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-object-legal-hold): Provides information about the Legal Hold status of a specific object that is already contained within the cloud.
- [Retrieve the ACL for a Specific Object in the Cloud](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-object-acl): Gives you information about the Access Control List (ACL) for a specific object within your Backblaze bucket.
- [Retrieve an Object From Your Backblaze Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-object): Allows you to retrieve an object from your bucket, which may include plenty of information about that object.
- [Retrieve the Versioning State of a Backblaze Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-bucket-versioning): Gathers information about the versioning state of a specified bucket, which lets you keep multiple versions of files.
- [S3 Get Bucket Logging](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-bucket-logging)
- [Return the Region of a Specific Backblaze Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-bucket-location): Retrieves information about the region that a specific bucket is located, as long as that information is available.
- [Retrieve Your Encryption Configuration for a Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-bucket-encryption): Retrieves information about the current encryption configuration for a specific bucket, which you can change if desired.
- [Retrieve the CORS Configuration for Your Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-bucket-cors): Retrieves the CORS configuration list for your bucket, which you can then use in future calls as necessary.
- [Retrieve Your Bucket ACL for a Backblaze Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-get-bucket-acl): Retrieves the Access Control List (ACL) for a bucket in XML format, so you can use it for other calls.
- [Delete Multiple Objects From Your Backblaze Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-delete-objects): Removes multiple objects at once from both your bucket and from the overall Backblaze storage cloud.
- [Delete an Object Entirely Froim Your Backblaze Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-delete-object): Removes an object not just from an individual bucket, but also from the Backblaze cloud storage entirely.
- [Delete the Default Encryption Configuration From Your Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-delete-bucket-encryption): Deletes default encryption configuration, which encrypts information before storing it on cloud servers, from a bucket.
- [Delete Your CORS Configuration From an Existing Bucket](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-delete-bucket-cors): Deletes the CORS configuration from your bucket. allowing you to either redo the configuration or leave it blank.
- [Delete an Existing Bucket Within Your Backblaze Account](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-delete-bucket): Deletes an existing bucket. The deleted bucket must not have any remaining files at the time of deletion.
- [Initiate a Multipart Upload for a Large File](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-create-multipart-upload): Creates a multipart upload, which you can use to upload multiple parts of a large file individually.
- [Create a Bucket Within Your Backblaze Cloud Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-create-bucket): Creates a new bucket with a globally unique name so you can store your future Backblaze files together.
- [Create a Copy of an Existing Object in Backblaze Storage](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-copy-object): Allows you to create a copy of an object within your Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage for editing, moving, and more.
- [Complete Your Multipart Upload to the Backblaze Cloud](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-complete-multipart-upload): Completes your multipart upload by taking the previously uploaded parts and assembling them into a single file.
- [Abort a Multipart Upload When Using S3 API](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/s3-abort-multipart-upload): Stops a multipart upload to the Backblaze cloud that is currently in the process of uploading or finishing.
- [Introduction to the S3-Compatible API](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/introduction-to-the-s3-compatible-api): With the S3-compatible API, if your application is already written to use the S3 API, Backblaze B2 Cloud Storage will work with minimal code changes on your end.
- [Introduction to API Documentation - B2 Native API](https://www.backblaze.com/apidocs/introduction-to-the-partner-api): The Backblaze B2 Native API allows you to access plenty of functionality. Here's what you should know.
